On February 1, 2023, the [Superstar Racing Experience](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Superstar_Racing_Experience) (SRX) announced that Hamlin would compete in the [2023 SRX Series](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_SRX_Series) opener at [Stafford Motor Speedway](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stafford_Motor_Speedway) on July 13.[^cite_note-123] He went on to win the event, which was shortened by lightning.[^cite_note-124][^cite_note-125]

## Owner career

Further information: [23XI Racing](65517461.md)

On September 21, 2020, Denny Hamlin and NBA Hall of Famer [Michael Jordan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jordan), longtime friends, announced the formation of [23XI Racing](65517461.md), a single-car team for the 2021 NASCAR Cup Series season. [Bubba Wallace](35761776.md) would drive the No. 23 car.[^cite_note-126] On October 4, 2021, Wallace secured his first career Cup Series victory at [Talladega](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_YellaWood_500), making history as the first African-American driver to win a Cup race since [Wendell Scott](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wendell_Scott) in 1963.

For the [2022 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_NASCAR_Cup_Series), the team expanded to a two-car operation with the addition of the No. 45, driven by [2004 NASCAR Cup Series Champion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2004_NASCAR_Nextel_Cup_Series) [Kurt Busch](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kurt_Busch), who brought [Monster Energy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Monster_Energy) as a sponsor.[^cite_note-127] On May 16, 2022, Kurt Busch delivered 23XI's second win at [Kansas Speedway](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_AdventHealth_400), leading 116 of 267 laps. Later that year, Wallace won the [second Kansas race](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_Hollywood_Casino_400) for the team that fall, driving the No. 45 in place of Busch, who was sidelined with concussion symptoms from a wreck at [Pocono](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_M\&M's_Fan_Appreciation_400). In doing so, Wallace became the first African-American driver to win multiple Cup Series races.

On July 12, 2022, 23XI Racing and [Toyota Racing Development](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toyota_Racing_Development) (TRD) announced that [Tyler Reddick](41108702.md) would join the team full-time in 2024.[^cite_note-128] However, following Busch's announcement on October 16 that he would step away from full-time racing in 2023, it was revealed that Reddick's contract with [Richard Childress Racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Richard_Childress_Racing) would be bought out by 23XI, and he would replace Busch for the 2023 season.

Reddick won the [2024 NASCAR Cup Series](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_NASCAR_Cup_Series) regular season championship, the first for the team.[^cite_note-Reddick-129] 23XI Racing acquired a third charter from the defunct [Stewart–Haas Racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stewart%E2%80%93Haas_Racing), the No. 35 driven by [Riley Herbst](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Riley_Herbst) beginning in [2025](77079684.md).[^cite_note-130] Wallace won the [2025 Brickyard 400](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Brickyard_400) for the team. Jordan, Hamlin, and his team, along with [Front Row Motorsports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Front_Row_Motorsports) [sued NASCAR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/23XI_Racing_v._NASCAR) in October 2024, over the new charter agreements;[^cite_note-131] the case was later settled on December 11, 2025, after 8 days in court.[^cite_note-132]

In 2026, Hamlin won his first Daytona 500 as an owner when Reddick won [the race](81427567.md).[^cite_note-133]

## In media

In 2016, Hamlin served as a guest analyst for [Fox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NASCAR_on_Fox) during the Xfinity Series race at Talladega. The following year, he returned to Fox for the Cup drivers-only broadcast of the Xfinity race at Pocono, where he worked from the Hollywood Hotel studio.[^cite_note-134]

In 2021, Hamlin appeared in the music video for [Post Malone's](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Post_Malone_discography) song "[Motley Crew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Motley_Crew_\(song\))," alongside driver [Bubba Wallace](35761776.md).[^cite_note-135]

In 2023, Denny Hamlin launched a weekly podcast with *Dirty Mo Media* called *Actions Detrimental*, which airs every Monday morning. Hamlin hosts the show alongside his co-host, social media manager, and friend, Jared Allen. The podcast features content from the latest Cup Series race, fan questions in a segment called "Dear Denny," and additional race insights from Hamlin.[^cite_note-ActionsDetrimental3-136]

In 2025, Hamlin joined the *[NASCAR on The CW](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NASCAR_on_The_CW)* crew as a guest analyst at the [Dover race](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_BetRivers_200). The following year, he joined the crew for [Charlotte I](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Charbroil_300) and [Atlanta II](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Focused_Health_250_\(Atlanta\)).[^cite_note-137]

### Video games

Hamlin, alongside his [Toyota](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toyota) teammates, recorded video clips demonstrating how to drive a lap at each of the NASCAR tracks featured in the 2016 circuit for the video game *[NASCAR Heat Evolution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NASCAR_Heat_Evolution)*. He was also one of eight playable drivers in the EA Sports *[NASCAR Racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NASCAR_Racing)* arcade game, notably the only rookie featured. In 2022, Hamlin appeared on the front cover of *[NASCAR Rivals](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NASCAR_Rivals)*, alongside his car.[^cite_note-138]

## Personal life

Hamlin is the youngest son of Dennis Hamlin and Mary Lou Clark. He was born in Tampa, Florida, at what is now St. Joseph's Women's Hospital, and moved to Virginia when he was two years old.[^cite_note-139] Denny's father, who was in the late stages of [COPD](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chronic_obstructive_pulmonary_disease), died from injuries sustained in a house fire on December 28, 2025, at the age of 75.

Hamlin and Jordan Fish have two daughters and a son together.[^cite_note-140][^cite_note-141][^cite_note-Albino–2025-109] On January 1, 2024, Hamlin and Fish became engaged.[^cite_note-142]

Hamlin is a basketball fan and has season tickets for the [Charlotte Hornets](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charlotte_Hornets) of the [National Basketball Association](22093.md) (NBA).[^cite_note-b442-143] Hamlin is also a recreational player and has torn the [anterior cruciate ligament](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anterior_cruciate_ligament) (ACL) in each of his knees once while playing, the left in 2010 and the right in 2015.[^cite_note-t457-144][^cite_note-j811-145]
